- **suba-acquisition**: Regency subagent of the DA role. Use when data must be brought in before anything else: extract from APIs, feeds or database endpoints into immutable landing artefacts with manifests and hashes, then profile the landed structure and report drift against the baseline. Moves bytes; never cleans, casts, models or analyses, and writes nowhere but the landing target its packet names. ([Trevojan/regency](https://github.com/Trevojan/regency/tree/HEAD/library/subagents/suba-acquisition) · ★1)
- **suba-analysis**: Regency subagent of the DA role. Use when a business question needs evidence: classify it as descriptive or causal, answer it with exactly the matching instrument - exploratory statistics or hypothesis testing, never merged - and return a bounded claim with its method, assumptions, confounders and residual uncertainty. Never told the hoped-for answer; never defines criteria, extracts or models data. ([Trevojan/regency](https://github.com/Trevojan/regency/tree/HEAD/library/subagents/suba-analysis) · ★1)
- **suba-architect**: Regency subagent of the DV role. Use when DV needs structure before a build: decompose the objective into components with responsibilities, boundaries, interfaces, dependencies and invariants, at the rung of DV's scale ladder the step asks for, and return the smallest sufficient architecture with its structural decisions as ADR drafts. Does not define requirements, write production code or verify. ([Trevojan/regency](https://github.com/Trevojan/regency/tree/HEAD/library/subagents/suba-architect) · ★1)
- **suba-design**: Regency subagent of the PO role. Use when the PO must specify what an experience permits: flows, every reachable state with its transitions, affordances and recovery, under the project's design language in its .ux/ folder, and a handoff of checkable conditions so nothing is left for the implementer to invent. Specifies intent only; never evaluates usability, writes criteria or tests, implements, or hands anything to DV or QA directly. ([Trevojan/regency](https://github.com/Trevojan/regency/tree/HEAD/library/subagents/suba-design) · ★1)
- **suba-implementer**: Regency subagent of the DV role. Use when DV delegates the build stage of a framed task: turn an accepted architecture, story or correction into the smallest working change inside the declared span, with the named test seen red then green, and return commit-ready change notes to DV. Does not define requirements, decide architecture, accept its own work or commit. ([Trevojan/regency](https://github.com/Trevojan/regency/tree/HEAD/library/subagents/suba-implementer) · ★1)
- **suba-modeling**: Regency subagent of the DA role. Use when landed data need durable structure or a binding guarantee: declare the grain, design facts and dimensions with their SCD strategy, define semantic metrics consistent with that grain, and write the data contract - name, trigger, payload, types, optionality, key, granularity - with lineage for every engineered feature. Never extracts, tests hypotheses or defines what a metric means to the business. ([Trevojan/regency](https://github.com/Trevojan/regency/tree/HEAD/library/subagents/suba-modeling) · ★1)
- **suba-recon**: Regency subagent of the QA role. Use when QA needs the verification context before judging: map the environment a change lives in - the norm slices that govern it, conventions, contracts, tooling, the test surface - compress recurring knowledge into pointers with provenance, and turn a verified finding into a correction brief in QA's bug-report shape. Read-only; never fixes, redesigns or sets severity. ([Trevojan/regency](https://github.com/Trevojan/regency/tree/HEAD/library/subagents/suba-recon) · ★1)
- **suba-requirements-analyst**: Regency subagent of the PO role. Use when the PO frames a story: bound the client's intent into a problem domain with explicit scope, surface assumptions and contradictions, and specify verifiable Given/When/Then criteria, invariants and state transitions, each traced to a briefing field, a recorded answer or a norm, with criticality and maximum tolerable severity. Never invents objectives, writes tests or designs systems; new rules leave as norm drafts. ([Trevojan/regency](https://github.com/Trevojan/regency/tree/HEAD/library/subagents/suba-requirements-analyst) · ★1)
- **suba-research**: Proposal - a Regency subagent of the PO role, drafted by an agent and awaiting the Regent's ratification. Use when the PO needs evidence about users before framing or after a build: a user model and goals traced to their sources, claims about users audited for support, closed questions for the client, and - when a built result is under review - where it failed its users' goals. Works only from the sources the PO's packet holds. ([Trevojan/regency](https://github.com/Trevojan/regency/tree/HEAD/library/subagents/suba-research) · ★1)
- **suba-scribe**: Regency subagent of the DV role, also called directly through /regency:scribe. Under DV it turns a concept, requirement or development problem into a critically examined, causally structured development artefact by chaining the inference engines inside DV's span. Called directly it writes a text for its caller - a document, spec, essay or chapter - with the same engines and none of DV's authority. One subagent, two jurisdictions. ([Trevojan/regency](https://github.com/Trevojan/regency/tree/HEAD/library/subagents/suba-scribe) · ★1)
- **suba-strategy**: Proposal - a Regency subagent of the PO role, drafted by an agent and awaiting the Regent's ratification. Use when the PO must decide what is worth building and in what order: the value hypothesis and success metric, scope cuts that keep MINIMUM strictly smaller than GRAND, modules ordered by real dependency, the smallest function rung that satisfies each rule, and the trade-offs between scope, deadline, debt and residual risk. Prepares the decision; the PO decides. ([Trevojan/regency](https://github.com/Trevojan/regency/tree/HEAD/library/subagents/suba-strategy) · ★1)
- **suba-tester**: Regency subagent of the QA role. Use when QA needs justified coverage: form a test strategy proportionate to risk - surfaces, techniques, the invariants worth defending, the coherence-matrix cases a change must keep - author the tests it implies as verbatim artefacts, confirm each named test fails when its behaviour is removed, and report as evidence only what actually ran. Does not judge criteria, classify defects, set severity or issue the version verdict. ([Trevojan/regency](https://github.com/Trevojan/regency/tree/HEAD/library/subagents/suba-tester) · ★1)
- **suba-validator**: Regency subagent of the QA role. Use when evidence exists and needs meaning: judge it against each stated criterion (met, not met, partially met, not verifiable), measure risk when it is not yet known, and classify verified failures against the coherence matrix - regression or new, type, severity input, priority - each keyed by its class. Does not gather evidence, rewrite criteria, write the correction brief or issue the version verdict. ([Trevojan/regency](https://github.com/Trevojan/regency/tree/HEAD/library/subagents/suba-validator) · ★1)

- **subaccount-roles-export**: Export application roles, groups, and user assignments from a Neo subaccount. - **subaccount-roles-import**: Assign Neo application roles and group memberships into a CF subaccount after apps are deployed. Reads ~/.neo-migration/$SUBACCOUNT/$CF_SUBACCOUNT_GUID/neo-roles.json (produced by subaccount-roles-export), resolves live XSUAA appIds from deployed CF apps, adds role-templates from those apps to the role collections created by authentication-xsuaa, and assigns users via BTP CLI. Consent files live under ~/.neo-migration-consents/$SUBACCOUNT/. Generates a manual checklist for unresolved apps, group role links, and the Everyone role. MUST run after all applications are deployed to CF. ([SAP-samples/btp-neo-java-app-migration](https://github.com/SAP-samples/btp-neo-java-app-migration/tree/HEAD/ai-migration/neo-java-migration-skills/skills/subaccount-roles-import) · ★6 · 2 copies)
- **subaccount-trust-migrator**: Migrate application IdP trust configuration from a Neo subaccount to Cloud Foundry in a single pass. Fetches the SAML trust config from Neo via the Trust Management REST API, classifies each identity provider as IAS or third-party, creates SAML trust in the CF subaccount via the XSUAA apiaccess REST API, and assigns assertion-based group rules via BTP CLI. Data flows entirely in memory — nothing is written to disk and the model never sees the raw trust configuration. Invoke when migrating IdP/trust configuration from Neo to Cloud Foundry as part of subaccount migration. ([SAP-samples/btp-neo-java-app-migration](https://github.com/SAP-samples/btp-neo-java-app-migration/tree/HEAD/ai-migration/neo-java-migration-skills/skills/subaccount-trust-migrator) · ★6 · 2 copies)
